Method: 提出LATENT系统，从非完美的人类动作数据中学习运动技能。这些数据仅包含捕捉网球基本技能的动作片段，而非完整精确的网球比赛动作序列。通过校正和组合这些准真实数据，学习人形机器人策略，使其能在各种条件下稳定击打来球并回击到目标位置，同时保持自然的运动风格。还提出了一系列鲁棒的sim-to-real迁移设计

Result: 该方法在真实世界中取得了令人惊讶的结果，能够在Unitree G1人形机器人上稳定地与人类玩家进行多拍对打

Conclusion: LATENT系统证明了从非完美的人类动作数据中学习人形机器人动态网球技能的可行性，通过准真实数据提供的人类基本技能先验，结合校正和组合技术，实现了鲁棒的sim-to-real迁移和稳定的多拍对打能力

Abstract: Human athletes demonstrate versatile and highly-dynamic tennis skills to successfully conduct competitive rallies with a high-speed tennis ball. However, reproducing such behaviors on humanoid robots is difficult, partially due to the lack of perfect humanoid action data or human kinematic motion data in tennis scenarios as reference. In this work, we propose LATENT, a system that Learns Athletic humanoid TEnnis skills from imperfect human motioN daTa. The imperfect human motion data consist only of motion fragments that capture the primitive skills used when playing tennis rather than precise and complete human-tennis motion sequences from real-world tennis matches, thereby significantly reducing the difficulty of data collection. Our key insight is that, despite being imperfect, such quasi-realistic data still provide priors about human primitive skills in tennis scenarios. With further correction and composition, we learn a humanoid policy that can consistently strike incoming balls under a wide range of conditions and return them to target locations, while preserving natural motion styles. We also propose a series of designs for robust sim-to-real transfer and deploy our policy on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot. Our method achieves surprising results in the real world and can stably sustain multi-shot rallies with human players. Project page: https://zzk273.github.io/LATENT/

</details>


### [81] [HaltNav: Reactive Visual Halting over Lightweight Topological Priors for Robust Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12696)
*Pingcong Li,Zihui Yu,Bichi Zhang,Sören Schwertfeger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HaltNav：基于osmAG拓扑地图和反应式视觉暂停的层次化视觉语言导航框架，结合全局规划与局部探索，提升长视野导航在环境变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言导航正从逐步指令跟随转向开放词汇的目标导向自主导航。现有方法依赖计算密集的2D/3D度量地图，而全局规划在真实环境中易受局部连接变化（如关闭的门、拥挤通道）影响导致执行失败，需要更轻量且鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 提出HaltNav层次化导航框架：1) 使用轻量文本型osmAG（OpenStreetMap Area Graph）作为拓扑表示；2) MLLM大脑模块负责高层任务理解和障碍感知，将全局路径转换为局部执行片段；3) 反应式视觉暂停机制检测局部异常，中断控制循环，更新osmAG拓扑并触发重规划；4) 数据合成管道利用生成模型注入真实障碍物，丰富困难负样本训练暂停能力。

Result: 实验表明该框架在无需繁琐语言指令的情况下优于多个基线方法，显著提升了长视野视觉语言导航在环境变化下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HaltNav通过结合osmAG的全局规划鲁棒性和VLN的局部探索能力，利用反应式视觉暂停机制处理环境变化，为开放词汇目标导向导航提供了轻量且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) is shifting from rigid, step-by-step instruction following toward open-vocabulary, goal-oriented autonomy. Achieving this transition without exhaustive routing prompts requires agents to leverage structural priors. While prior work often assumes computationally heavy 2D/3D metric maps, we instead exploit a lightweight, text-based osmAG (OpenStreetMap Area Graph), a floorplan-level topological representation that is easy to obtain and maintain. However, global planning over a prior map alone is brittle in real-world deployments, where local connectivity can change (e.g., closed doors or crowded passages), leading to execution-time failures. To address this gap, we propose a hierarchical navigation framework HaltNav that couples the robust global planning of osmAG with the local exploration and instruction-grounding capability of VLN. Our approach features an MLLM-based brain module, which is capable of high-level task grounding and obstruction awareness. Conditioned on osmAG, the brain converts the global route into a sequence of localized execution snippets, providing the VLN executor with prior-grounded, goal-centric sub-instructions. Meanwhile, it detects local anomalies via a mechanism we term Reactive Visual Halting (RVH), which interrupts the local control loop, updates osmAG by invalidating the corresponding topology, and triggers replanning to orchestrate a viable detour. To train this halting capability efficiently, we introduce a data synthesis pipeline that leverages generative models to inject realistic obstacles into otherwise navigable scenes, substantially enriching hard negative samples. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our hierarchical framework outperforms several baseline methods without tedious language instructions, and significantly improves robustness for long-horizon vision-language navigation under environmental changes.

</details>


### [82] [Altered Thoughts, Altered Actions: Probing Chain-of-Thought Vulnerabilities in VLA Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12717)
*Tuan Duong Trinh,Naveed Akhtar,Basim Azam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现VLA模型中的内部推理轨迹存在安全漏洞：简单的对象名称替换攻击能显著降低机器人任务成功率，而更复杂的语义攻击反而影响有限，表明动作解码器主要依赖实体引用完整性而非推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作模型普遍采用思维链推理，在解码运动命令前生成自然语言计划。这种推理模块与动作解码器之间的内部文本通道尚未受到对抗性攻击的审视。研究旨在探究动作解码器实际依赖推理轨迹的哪些属性，以及仅针对推理轨迹的有针对性破坏（保持所有输入完整）是否会影响机器人的物理任务性能。

Method: 设计了七种文本破坏方法的分类体系，分为三个攻击者层级（盲噪声、机械语义和LLM自适应攻击），并将其应用于最先进的推理VLA模型，在40个LIBERO桌面操作任务上进行测试。同时使用非推理VLA模型进行跨架构对照实验。

Result: 结果显示出显著不对称性：替换推理轨迹中的对象名称使总体成功率降低8.3个百分点（在目标条件任务中达到-19.3pp，在个别任务中达到-45pp），而句子重排序、空间方向反转、令牌噪声甚至由70B参数LLM生成的看似合理但错误的计划都影响有限（在±4pp内）。这表明动作解码器依赖实体引用完整性而非推理质量或顺序结构。值得注意的是，复杂的基于LLM的攻击者表现不如简单的机械对象名称替换，因为保持合理性无意中保留了解码器所需的实体接地结构。

Conclusion: 跨架构对照实验证实该漏洞是推理增强模型独有的，而指令级攻击会同时影响两种架构，表明内部推理轨迹是一个独特且隐蔽的威胁向量，对输入验证防御不可见。这揭示了VLA模型安全性的新维度，即内部推理通道可能成为攻击目标。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly adopt chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, generating a natural-language plan before decoding motor commands. This internal text channel between the reasoning module and the action decoder has received no adversarial scrutiny. We ask: which properties of this intermediate plan does the action decoder actually rely on, and can targeted corruption of the reasoning trace alone -- with all inputs left intact -- degrade a robot's physical task performance? We design a taxonomy of seven text corruptions organized into three attacker tiers (blind noise, mechanical-semantic, and LLM-adaptive) and apply them to a state-of-the-art reasoning VLA across 40 LIBERO tabletop manipulation tasks. Our results reveal a striking asymmetry: substituting object names in the reasoning trace reduces overall success rate by 8.3~percentage points (pp) -- reaching $-$19.3~pp on goal-conditioned tasks and $-$45~pp on individual tasks -- whereas sentence reordering, spatial-direction reversal, token noise, and even a 70B-parameter LLM crafting plausible-but-wrong plans all have negligible impact (within $\pm$4~pp). This asymmetry indicates that the action decoder depends on entity-reference integrity rather than reasoning quality or sequential structure. Notably, a sophisticated LLM-based attacker underperforms simple mechanical object-name substitution, because preserving plausibility inadvertently retains the entity-grounding structure the decoder needs. A cross-architecture control using a non-reasoning VLA confirms the vulnerability is exclusive to reasoning-augmented models, while instruction-level attacks degrade both architectures -- establishing that the internal reasoning trace is a distinct and stealthy threat vector invisible to input-validation defenses.

</details>


### [83] [AnchorVLA4D: an Anchor-Based Spatial-Temporal Vision-Language-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12730)
*Juan Zhu,Zhanying Shao,Xiaoqi Li,Ethan Morgan,Jiadong Xu,Hongwei Fan,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnchorVLA4D通过引入锚点图像和轻量级空间编码器，增强视觉-语言-动作系统的时空推理能力，无需额外传感器且推理开销小


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA系统存在空间感知有限和缺乏记忆的问题，导致在遮挡下容易遗忘物体并在操作过程中空间迷失

Method: 提出AnchorVLA4D，在视觉输入中增加锚点图像以保留初始场景上下文，并添加轻量级空间编码器联合处理锚点和当前帧以暴露几何关系

Result: 在Simpler WidowX基准测试中提升13.6%，真实世界任务平均成功率80%，无需深度或点云等额外传感器

Conclusion: 锚点机制与空间编码器结合能有效增强VLA系统的时空推理能力，显著提升机器人操作性能

Abstract: Since current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems suffer from limited spatial perception and the absence of memory throughout manipulation, we investigate visual anchors as a means to enhance spatial and temporal reasoning within VLA policies for robotic manipulation. Conventional VLAs generate actions by conditioning on a single current frame together with a language instruction. However, since the frame is encoded as a 2D image, it does not contain detailed spatial information, and the VLA similarly lacks any means to incorporate past context. As a result, it frequently forgets objects under occlusion and becomes spatially disoriented during the manipulation process. Thus, we propose AnchorVLA4D, a simple spatial-temporal VLA that augments the visual input with an anchor image to preserve the initial scene context throughout execution, and adds a lightweight spatial encoder that jointly processes the anchor and current frames to expose geometric relationships within an episode. Built on a Qwen2.5-VL backbone with a diffusion-based action head, AnchorVLA4D requires no additional sensing modalities (e.g., depth or point clouds) and introduces negligible inference overhead. Combining anchoring with a frozen pretrained spatial encoder yields further gains, realizing a 13.6% improvement on the Simpler WidowX benchmark and confirming the approach on real-world tasks, where it achieved an average success rate of 80%.

</details>


### [84] [Easy-IIL: Reducing Human Operational Burden in Interactive Imitation Learning via Assistant Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12769)
*Chengjie Zhang,Chao Tang,Wenlong Dong,Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Easy-IIL框架使用现成的基于模型的模仿学习方法作为辅助专家，替代人类进行大部分数据收集，只需人类提供单次演示初始化辅助专家，并在关键状态进行干预，显著减少人类操作负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交互式模仿学习需要大量人类参与，包括离线演示和在线交互。现有工作主要关注减少被动监控而非主动操作的人力。基于模型的模仿方法在低数据量时表现与端到端方法相当但需要更少演示，但数据增加后通常被超越。利用这一洞察，希望减少人类主动操作负担。

Method: 提出Easy-IIL框架：1) 人类专家提供单次演示初始化辅助专家；2) 使用现成的基于模型模仿方法作为辅助专家替代人类进行大部分数据收集；3) 人类仅在任务接近失败的关键状态进行干预；4) 通过保持离线和在线数据质量来维持IIL性能。

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验表明，Easy-IIL显著减少人类操作负担，同时保持与主流IIL基线相当的性能。用户研究进一步证实Easy-IIL减少了人类专家的主观工作负荷。

Conclusion: Easy-IIL通过利用基于模型的模仿方法作为辅助专家，有效减少了交互式模仿学习中人类主动操作的负担，同时保持了学习性能，为减少人类参与提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Interactive Imitation Learning (IIL) typically relies on extensive human involvement for both offline demonstration and online interaction. Prior work primarily focuses on reducing human effort in passive monitoring rather than active operation. Interestingly, structured model-based imitation approaches achieve comparable performance with significantly fewer demonstrations than end-to-end imitation learning policies in the low-data regime. However, these methods are typically surpassed by end-to-end policies as the data increases. Leveraging this insight, we propose Easy-IIL, a framework that utilizes off-the-shelf model-based imitation methods as an assistant expert to replace active human operation for the majority of data collection. The human expert only provides a single demonstration to initialize the assistant expert and intervenes in critical states where the task is approaching failure. Furthermore, Easy-IIL can maintain IIL performance by preserving both offline and online data quality. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Easy-IIL significantly reduces human operational burden while maintaining performance comparable to mainstream IIL baselines. User studies further confirm that Easy-IIL reduces subjective workload on the human expert. Project page: https://sites.google.com/view/easy-iil

</details>


### [85] [Motion-Specific Battery Health Assessment for Quadrotors Using High-Fidelity Battery Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12791)
*Joonhee Kim,Sanghyun Park,Donghyeong Kim,Eunseon Choi,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个端到端框架，用于四旋翼飞行器的运动感知电池健康评估，通过捕捉飞行运动中的瞬态电流特征，结合高保真电池模型分析不同飞行动作如何导致不同的电池退化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼飞行器的续航能力最终受限于电池性能，但大多数能量感知规划将电池视为简单的能量储存器，忽略了飞行运动引起的动态电流负载会加速电池退化的问题。

Method: 1) 设计宽范围电流传感模块捕捉真实飞行中的运动特定电流剖面；2) 使用参考性能测试和基于退化耦合电化学模型的元启发式方法校准高保真电池模型；3) 通过模拟测量的飞行负载，系统分析不同飞行动作如何转化为退化模式。

Result: 研究结果表明，即使两种飞行剖面消耗相同的平均能量，它们的瞬态负载结构也会驱动不同的退化路径（锂库存损失、活性材料损失以及内部副反应），强调了运动感知电池管理的必要性。

Conclusion: 需要开发能够平衡效率和电池退化的运动感知电池管理系统，因为飞行运动的瞬态负载特征对电池健康有显著影响，而传统方法忽略了这一关键因素。

Abstract: Quadrotor endurance is ultimately limited by battery behavior, yet most energy aware planning treats the battery as a simple energy reservoir and overlooks how flight motions induce dynamic current loads that accelerate battery degradation. This work presents an end to end framework for motion aware battery health assessment in quadrotors. We first design a wide range current sensing module to capture motion specific current profiles during real flights, preserving transient features. In parallel, a high fidelity battery model is calibrated using reference performance tests and a metaheuristic based on a degradation coupled electrochemical model.By simulating measured flight loads in the calibrated model, we systematically resolve how different flight motions translate into degradation modes loss of lithium inventory and loss of active material as well as internal side reactions. The results demonstrate that even when two flight profiles consume the same average energy, their transient load structures can drive different degradation pathways, emphasizing the need for motion-aware battery management that balances efficiency with battery degradation.

</details>


### [86] [FLUX: Accelerating Cross-Embodiment Generative Navigation Policies via Rectified Flow and Static-to-Dynamic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12806)
*Zeying Gong,Yangyi Zhong,Yiyi Ding,Tianshuai Hu,Guoyang Zhao,Lingdong Kong,Rong Li,Jiadi You,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLUX：首个基于流的统一导航策略，通过线性化概率流实现高效推理，在动态导航基准DynBench上实现SOTA性能，并具备零样本跨平台迁移能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主导航评估存在碎片化问题，静态目标到达和动态社交穿越等不同技能需要在不同协议下评估，缺乏统一的评估框架。同时，现有导航方法在推理效率和统一策略方面存在不足。

Method: 提出DynBench动态导航基准，结合现有静态协议形成六项基础导航任务的综合评估框架。在此基础上开发FLUX策略：1）通过线性化概率流，用直线轨迹替代迭代去噪，提升推理效率；2）采用静态到动态的课程学习策略，先建立几何先验，再通过强化学习在动态社交环境中精炼；3）利用随机动作分布捕捉恢复行为。

Result: FLUX相比先前基于流的方法提升单步推理效率47%，相比基于扩散的方法提升29%。在所有六项导航任务中均达到最先进性能，并在轮式、四足和人形平台上实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，无需微调。

Conclusion: FLUX作为首个基于流的统一导航策略，通过高效的线性化概率流和课程学习框架，实现了跨任务和跨平台的卓越性能，为自主导航提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation requires a broad spectrum of skills, from static goal-reaching to dynamic social traversal, yet evaluation remains fragmented across disparate protocols. We introduce DynBench, a dynamic navigation benchmark featuring physically valid crowd simulation. Combined with existing static protocols, it supports comprehensive evaluation across six fundamental navigation tasks. Within this framework, we propose FLUX, the first flow-based unified navigation policy. By linearizing probability flow, FLUX replaces iterative denoising with straight-line trajectories, improving per-step inference efficiency by 47% over prior flow-based methods and 29% over diffusion-based ones. Following a static-to-dynamic curriculum, FLUX initially establishes geometric priors and is subsequently refined through reinforcement learning in dynamic social environments. This regime not only strengthens socially-aware navigation but also enhances static task robustness by capturing recovery behaviors through stochastic action distributions. FLUX achieves state-of-the-art performance across all tasks and demonstrates zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on wheeled, quadrupedal, and humanoid platforms without any fine-tuning.

</details>


### [87] [SmoothTurn: Learning to Turn Smoothly for Agile Navigation with Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12842)
*Zunzhi You,Haolan Guo,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmoothTurn：一种用于四足机器人敏捷顺序局部导航的学习控制框架，通过顺序目标到达奖励、前瞻窗口观察和自动目标课程学习，实现高速奔跑时的平滑转向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目标导航方法在顺序目标导航中无法预判即将到来的机动动作或保持目标切换时的动量，限制了四足机器人在消防救援、工业巡检等紧急应用中的敏捷导航潜力

Method: 将任务形式化为顺序局部导航，提出SmoothTurn框架：采用顺序目标到达奖励、扩展包含未来目标前瞻窗口的观察空间，以及基于目标到达性能逐步增加难度的自动目标课程学习

Result: 训练出的策略可直接部署在配备机载传感器和计算设备的真实四足机器人上，仿真和真实世界实验显示策略能实现跨目标的平滑转向，涌现出控制动量、提前面向未来目标和规划高效路径等行为

Conclusion: SmoothTurn框架成功解决了顺序目标导航中的平滑转向问题，使四足机器人能够充分发挥其敏捷性潜力，在高速奔跑时实现方向切换，适用于需要紧急敏捷导航的实际应用场景

Abstract: Quadrupedal robots show great potential for valuable real-world applications such as fire rescue and industrial inspection. Such applications often require urgency and the ability to navigate agilely, which in turn demands the capability to change directions smoothly while running in high speed. Existing approaches for agile navigation typically learn a single-goal reaching policy by encouraging the robot to stay at the target position after reaching there. As a result, when the policy is used to reach sequential goals that require changing directions, it cannot anticipate upcoming maneuvers or maintain momentum across the switch of goals, thereby preventing the robot from fully exploiting its agility potential. In this work, we formulate the task as sequential local navigation, extending the single-goal-conditioned local navigation formulation in prior work. We then introduce SmoothTurn, a learning-based control framework that learns to turn smoothly while running rapidly for agile sequential local navigation. The framework adopts a novel sequential goal-reaching reward, an expanded observation space with a lookahead window for future goals, and an automatic goal curriculum that progressively expands the difficulty of sampled goal sequences based on the goal-reaching performance. The trained policy can be directly deployed on real quadrupedal robots with onboard sensors and computation. Both simulation and real-world empirical results show that SmoothTurn learns an agile locomotion policy that performs smooth turning across goals, with emergent behaviors such as controlling momentum when switching goals, facing towards the future goal in advance, and planning efficient paths. We have provided video demos of the learned motions in the supplementary materials. The source code and trained policies will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [88] [Consistent and Efficient MSCKF-based LiDAR-Inertial Odometry with Inferred Cluster-to-Plane Constraints for UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12904)
*Jinwen Zhu,Xudong Zhao,Fangcheng Zhu,Jun Hu,Shi Jin,Yinian Mao,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种针对无人机的高效紧耦合激光雷达-惯性里程计框架，通过空域投影消除特征参数依赖，采用并行体素数据关联和紧凑聚类-平面测量模型，在保持精度的同时显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机导航对精度和鲁棒性要求高，但现有激光雷达-惯性里程计系统在尺寸、重量和功率受限平台上存在估计不一致性和计算瓶颈问题，需要专门针对无人机平台的解决方案。

Method: 基于高效多状态约束卡尔曼滤波器框架，构建滑动窗口内的平面特征共面约束，通过空域投影消除状态向量中特征参数的直接依赖；引入并行体素数据关联和紧凑聚类-平面测量模型，无损降低观测维度以加速更新过程。

Result: 方法在一致性和效率方面优于多数先进方法，在退化场景中表现出更好的鲁棒性，通过无地图特性实现最低内存使用，可在资源受限的嵌入式平台（如NVIDIA Jetson TX2）上实时运行。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧耦合激光雷达-惯性里程计框架为无人机平台提供了高一致性、高效率的导航解决方案，特别适合尺寸、重量和功率受限的应用场景，在嵌入式平台上实现了实时性能。

Abstract: Robust and accurate navigation is critical for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) especially for those with stringent Size, Weight, and Power (SWaP) constraints. However, most state-of-the-art (SOTA) LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (LIO) systems still suffer from estimation inconsistency and computational bottlenecks when deployed on such platforms. To address these issues, this paper proposes a consistent and efficient tightly-coupled LIO framework tailored for UAVs. Within the efficient Multi-State Constraint Kalman Filter (MSCKF) framework, we build coplanar constraints inferred from planar features observed across a sliding window. By applying null-space projection to sliding-window coplanar constraints, we eliminate the direct dependency on feature parameters in the state vector, thereby mitigating overconfidence and improving consistency. More importantly, to further boost the efficiency, we introduce a parallel voxel-based data association and a novel compact cluster-to-plane measurement model. This compact measurement model losslessly reduces observation dimensionality and significantly accelerating the update process. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our method outperforms most state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches by providing a superior balance of consistency and efficiency. It exhibits improved robustness in degenerate scenarios, achieves the lowest memory usage via its map-free nature, and runs in real-time on resource-constrained embedded platforms (e.g., NVIDIA Jetson TX2).

</details>


### [89] [GoalSwarm: Multi-UAV Semantic Coordination for Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12908)
*MoniJesu Wonders James,Amir Atef Habel,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserokou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoalSwarm是一个完全去中心化的多无人机框架，用于零样本语义目标导航，通过共享轻量级2D语义占据地图、零样本基础模型和贝叶斯价值地图实现开放词汇目标识别和高效协作探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人团队在未知环境中进行协作视觉语义导航面临两大挑战：重型感知模型在机载设备上的计算限制，以及去中心化多智能体协调的复杂性。现有方法难以实现鲁棒的开放词汇目标导航。

Method: 1. 构建共享的轻量级2D顶部语义占据地图，通过投影深度观测保留几何和语义结构；2. 集成零样本基础模型SAM3进行开放词汇检测和像素级分割；3. 设计贝叶斯价值地图融合多视角检测置信度；4. 采用结合语义边界提取、成本效用投标和空间分离惩罚的去中心化协调策略。

Result: GoalSwarm框架实现了零样本语义目标导航，无需任务特定训练即可识别开放词汇目标，通过贝叶斯价值地图和UCB探索实现智能边界评分，并通过去中心化协调最小化群体冗余探索。

Conclusion: GoalSwarm为多无人机系统提供了一种计算高效、完全去中心化的开放词汇语义导航解决方案，通过轻量级2D表示、零样本感知和智能协调策略，克服了现有方法在计算和协调方面的限制。

Abstract: Cooperative visual semantic navigation is a foundational capability for aerial robot teams operating in unknown environments. However, achieving robust open-vocabulary object-goal navigation remains challenging due to the computational constraints of deploying heavy perception models onboard and the complexity of decentralized multi-agent coordination. We present GoalSwarm, a fully decentralized multi-UAV framework for zero-shot semantic object-goal navigation. Each UAV collaboratively constructs a shared, lightweight 2D top-down semantic occupancy map by projecting depth observations from aerial vantage points, eliminating the computational burden of full 3D representations while preserving essential geometric and semantic structure. The core contributions of GoalSwarm are threefold: (1) integration of zero-shot foundation model -- SAM3 for open vocabulary detection and pixel-level segmentation, enabling open-vocabulary target identification without task-specific training; (2) a Bayesian Value Map that fuses multi-viewpoint detection confidences into a per-pixel goal-relevance distribution, enabling informed frontier scoring via Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) exploration; and (3) a decentralized coordination strategy combining semantic frontier extraction, cost-utility bidding with geodesic path costs, and spatial separation penalties to minimize redundant exploration across the swarm.

</details>


### [90] [MotionAnymesh: Physics-Grounded Articulation for Simulation-Ready Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12936)
*WenBo Xu,Liu Liu,Li Zhang,Dan Guo,RuoNan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionAnymesh：一个零样本自动化框架，将静态3D网格转换为可交互的铰接资产，通过物理基础消除运动学幻觉，确保无碰撞铰接


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本流水线在处理复杂资产时缺乏物理基础，导致运动学幻觉和网格穿透问题，限制了在具身AI和机器人仿真中的应用

Method: 1) 运动学感知部件分割模块：将VLM推理与显式SP4D物理先验结合；2) 几何-物理联合估计流水线：结合类型感知初始化和物理约束轨迹优化

Result: 在几何精度和动态物理可执行性方面显著优于现有基线，为下游应用提供高可靠性资产

Conclusion: MotionAnymesh成功解决了静态网格到仿真就绪数字孪生的转换问题，通过物理基础方法消除了运动学幻觉，确保了无碰撞铰接

Abstract: Converting static 3D meshes into interactable articulated assets is crucial for embodied AI and robotic simulation. However, existing zero-shot pipelines struggle with complex assets due to a critical lack of physical grounding. Specifically, ungrounded Vision-Language Models (VLMs) frequently suffer from kinematic hallucinations, while unconstrained joint estimation inevitably leads to catastrophic mesh inter-penetration during physical simulation. To bridge this gap, we propose MotionAnymesh, an automated zero-shot framework that seamlessly transforms unstructured static meshes into simulation-ready digital twins. Our method features a kinematic-aware part segmentation module that grounds VLM reasoning with explicit SP4D physical priors, effectively eradicating kinematic hallucinations. Furthermore, we introduce a geometry-physics joint estimation pipeline that combines robust type-aware initialization with physics-constrained trajectory optimization to rigorously guarantee collision-free articulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MotionAnymesh significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both geometric precision and dynamic physical executability, providing highly reliable assets for downstream applications.

</details>


### [91] [RoboStream: Weaving Spatio-Temporal Reasoning with Memory in Vision-Language Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12939)
*Yuzhi Huang,Jie Wu,Weijue Bu,Ziyi Xiong,Gaoyang Jiang,Ye Li,Kangye Ji,Shuzhao Xie,Yue Huang,Chenglei Wu,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboStream：通过时空融合令牌和因果时空图实现几何锚定与因果记忆，无需训练即可显著提升长时程机器人操作的可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM规划器将每一步视为孤立的观察-动作映射，缺乏时空推理能力，无法持续跟踪几何关系和动作引发的状态变化，导致感知误差累积、物体遗忘和级联失败

Method: 提出RoboStream框架：1) 时空融合令牌(STF-Tokens)将视觉证据绑定到3D几何属性实现持久物体锚定；2) 因果时空图(CSTG)记录跨步骤的动作触发状态转换，保持因果连续性

Result: 在长时程RLBench上达到90.5%成功率，在真实世界积木搭建任务上达到44.4%，而SoFar和VoxPoser仅为11.1%，证明了时空推理和因果记忆对可靠长时程操作的重要性

Conclusion: 时空推理和因果记忆是实现可靠长时程机器人操作的关键缺失组件，RoboStream通过几何锚定和因果连续性跟踪，无需额外训练即可显著提升性能

Abstract: Enabling reliable long-horizon robotic manipulation is a crucial step toward open-world embodied intelligence. However, VLM-based planners treat each step as an isolated observation-to-action mapping, forcing them to reinfer scene geometry from raw pixels at every decision point while remaining unaware of how prior actions have reshaped the environment. Despite strong short-horizon performance, these systems lack the spatio-temporal reasoning required for persistent geometric anchoring and memory of action-triggered state transitions. Without persistent state tracking, perceptual errors accumulate across the execution horizon, temporarily occluded objects are catastrophically forgotten, and these compounding failures lead to precondition violations that cascade through subsequent steps. In contrast, humans maintain a persistent mental model that continuously tracks spatial relations and action consequences across interactions rather than reconstructing them at each instant. Inspired by this human capacity for causal spatio-temporal reasoning with persistent memory, we propose RoboStream, a training-free framework that achieves geometric anchoring through Spatio-Temporal Fusion Tokens (STF-Tokens), which bind visual evidence to 3D geometric attributes for persistent object grounding, and maintains causal continuity via a Causal Spatio-Temporal Graph (CSTG) that records action-triggered state transitions across steps. This design enables the planner to trace causal chains and preserve object permanence under occlusion without additional training or fine-tuning. RoboStream achieves 90.5% on long-horizon RLBench and 44.4% on challenging real-world block-building tasks, where both SoFar and VoxPoser score 11.1%, demonstrating that spatio-temporal reasoning and causal memory are critical missing components for reliable long-horizon manipulation.

</details>


### [92] [Coordinated Manipulation of Hybrid Deformable-Rigid Objects in Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12940)
*Anees Peringal,Anup Teejo Mathew,Panagiotis liatsis,Federico Renda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于应变Cosserat杆模型的准静态优化规划器，用于混合刚柔线性物体在受限环境中的机器人操作，通过解析梯度实现33倍加速，实验验证平均变形误差约3厘米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合刚柔线性物体（如含刚性元件的电缆）在受限环境中的协调操作具有挑战性，现有方法难以同时处理变形和刚性元素，需要开发能够利用柔性元件顺应性实现任务空间目标的规划方法。

Method: 采用基于应变的Cosserat杆模型扩展刚体公式到混合刚柔线性物体，开发准静态优化规划器，利用解析导数实现高效逆运动静力学求解，并通过轨迹优化（以IKS解为热启动）生成可行操作轨迹。

Result: 解析梯度方法比有限差分基线在逆运动静力学问题上实现高达33倍加速；实验验证中，三连杆混合刚柔线性物体在双臂机器人系统中操作，平均变形误差约3厘米（占柔性连杆长度的5%）；优化规划器优于基于采样的可行性规划器。

Conclusion: 提出的优化规划器能有效处理混合刚柔线性物体在受限环境中的操作问题，通过利用柔性元件的顺应性实现刚性工具无法达到的任务目标，解析导数对实际可行性至关重要，方法具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Coordinated robotic manipulation of deformable linear objects (DLOs), such as ropes and cables, has been widely studied; however, handling hybrid assemblies composed of both deformable and rigid elements in constrained environments remains challenging. This work presents a quasi-static optimization-based manipulation planner that employs a strain-based Cosserat rod model, extending rigid-body formulations to hybrid deformable linear objects (hDLO). The proposed planner exploits the compliance of deformable links to maneuver through constraints while achieving task-space objectives for the object that are unreachable with rigid tools. By leveraging a differentiable model with analytically derived gradients, the method achieves up to a 33x speedup over finite-difference baselines for inverse kinetostatic(IKS) problems. Furthermore, the subsequent trajectory optimization problem, warm-started using the IKS solution, is only practically realizable via analytical derivatives. The proposed algorithm is validated in simulation on various hDLO systems and experimentally on a three-link hDLO manipulated in a constrained environment using a dual-arm robotic system. Experimental results confirm the planner's accuracy, yielding an average deformation error of approximately 3 cm (5% of the deformable link length) between the desired and measured marker positions. Finally, the proposed optimal planner is compared against a sampling-based feasibility planner adapted to the strain-based formulation. The results demonstrate the effectiveness and applicability of the proposed approach for robotic manipulation of hybrid assemblies in constrained environments.

</details>


### [93] [ReMem-VLA: Empowering Vision-Language-Action Model with Memory via Dual-Level Recurrent Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12942)
*Hang Li,Fengyi Shen,Dong Chen,Liudi Yang,Xudong Wang,Jinkui Shi,Zhenshan Bing,Ziyuan Liu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReMem-VLA是一种具有循环记忆能力的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过可学习的查询机制解决机器人控制中的长期记忆问题，显著提升记忆依赖任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型基于马尔可夫假设，在处理需要历史上下文的任务时容易出错。现有记忆方法存在局限性：基于记忆库检索的方法易受干扰项误导，而扩展帧窗口的方法因固定时间范围限制长期记忆保留。

Method: 提出ReMem-VLA模型，包含两套可学习查询：帧级循环记忆查询用于在连续帧间传播信息支持短期记忆，块级循环记忆查询用于在时间块间传递上下文支持长期记忆。同时引入过去观察预测作为辅助训练目标增强视觉记忆。

Result: ReMem-VLA在记忆中心化仿真和真实机器人实验中展现出强大的多维度记忆能力（空间、序列、情景、时间、视觉记忆）。显著优于无记忆VLA基线π0.5和OpenVLA-OFT，并在记忆依赖任务上大幅超越MemoryVLA。

Conclusion: ReMem-VLA通过端到端训练的循环记忆查询机制有效解决了VLA模型中的长期记忆问题，无需额外训练或推理成本，为机器人控制提供了强大的记忆能力支持。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models for closed-loop robot control are typically cast under the Markov assumption, making them prone to errors on tasks requiring historical context. To incorporate memory, existing VLAs either retrieve from a memory bank, which can be misled by distractors, or extend the frame window, whose fixed horizon still limits long-term retention. In this paper, we introduce ReMem-VLA, a Recurrent Memory VLA model equipped with two sets of learnable queries: frame-level recurrent memory queries for propagating information across consecutive frames to support short-term memory, and chunk-level recurrent memory queries for carrying context across temporal chunks for long-term memory. These queries are trained end-to-end to aggregate and maintain relevant context over time, implicitly guiding the model's decisions without additional training or inference cost. Furthermore, to enhance visual memory, we introduce Past Observation Prediction as an auxiliary training objective. Through extensive memory-centric simulation and real-world robot experiments, we demonstrate that ReMem-VLA exhibits strong memory capabilities across multiple dimensions, including spatial, sequential, episodic, temporal, and visual memory. ReMem-VLA significantly outperforms memory-free VLA baselines $π$0.5 and OpenVLA-OFT and surpasses MemoryVLA on memory-dependent tasks by a large margin.

</details>


### [94] [Language-Grounded Decoupled Action Representation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12967)
*Wuding Weng,Tongshu Wu,Liucheng Chen,Siyu Xie,Zheng Wang,Xing Xu,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaDA框架通过语言引导的解耦动作表示，使用翻译、旋转和夹爪控制三个可解释动作基元作为中间层，通过语义引导的对比学习增强泛化能力，在机器人操作任务中实现更好的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高层视觉语言理解与低层动作控制之间的异质性仍然是机器人操作的基本挑战。现有方法在任务特定动作对齐方面有所进展，但在处理新颖或语义相关任务时往往难以生成鲁棒准确的动作。

Method: 提出语言引导的解耦动作表示（LaDA）框架：1）引入三个可解释动作基元（平移、旋转、夹爪控制）作为细粒度中间层；2）采用语义引导的软标签对比学习目标，对齐跨任务的相似动作基元；3）基于课程学习的自适应加权策略，动态平衡对比学习和模仿学习目标。

Result: 在模拟基准（LIBERO和MimicGen）和真实世界演示上的广泛实验验证了LaDA的强大性能，能够有效泛化到未见或相关任务。

Conclusion: LaDA框架通过语言作为语义桥梁连接感知与控制，使用可解释动作基元和语义引导对比学习，解决了机器人操作中视觉语言理解与动作控制之间的异质性挑战，实现了更好的泛化能力。

Abstract: The heterogeneity between high-level vision-language understanding and low-level action control remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation. Although recent methods have advanced task-specific action alignment, they often struggle to generate robust and accurate actions for novel or semantically related tasks. To address this, we propose the Language-Grounded Decoupled Action Representation (LaDA) framework, which leverages natural language as a semantic bridge to connect perception and control. LaDA introduces a fine-grained intermediate layer of three interpretable action primitives--translation, rotation, and gripper control--providing explicit semantic structure for low-level actions. It further employs a semantic-guided soft-label contrastive learning objective to align similar action primitives across tasks, enhancing generalization and motion consistency. An adaptive weighting strategy, inspired by curriculum learning, dynamically balances contrastive and imitation objectives for stable and effective training. Extensive experiments on simulated benchmarks (LIBERO and MimicGen) and real-world demonstrations validate that LaDA achieves strong performance and generalizes effectively to unseen or related tasks.

</details>


### [95] [Route Fragmentation Based on Resource-centric Prioritisation for Efficient Multi-Robot Path Planning in Agricultural Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12994)
*James R. Heselden,Gautham P. Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 资源中心的多机器人路径规划算法Fragment Planner在农业走廊环境中通过路线分段实现高吞吐量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业环境中存在大量空间密集的导航瓶颈，现有以智能体为中心的多机器人路径规划方法从智能体角度而非资源竞争角度解决冲突，且空间交错能力受限于高密度竞争

Method: 提出了两种基于优先级的Fragment Planner变体作为资源中心MRPP算法，利用路线分段实现部分路线推进，限制基于二进制的等待影响

Result: 在3.6公里拓扑地图的商业隧道环境中进行终身模拟，与5种基线算法对比，Fragment Planner相比优先规划和基于优先级的搜索算法获得显著吞吐量提升，任务吞吐量达到同期最优任务吞吐量的95%

Conclusion: 对于在走廊主导的农业环境中长期部署农业机器人，资源中心的多机器人路径规划方法是实现高效运营规划的必要条件

Abstract: Agricultural environments present high proportions of spatially dense navigation bottlenecks for long-term navigation and operational planning of agricultural mobile robots. The existing agent-centric multi-robot path planning (MRPP) approaches resolve conflicts from the perspective of agents, rather than from the resources under contention. Further, the density of such contentions limits the capabilities of spatial interleaving, a concept that many planners rely on to achieve high throughput. In this work, two variants of the priority-based Fragment Planner (FP) are presented as resource-centric MRPP algorithms that leverage route fragmentation to enable partial route progression and limit the impact of binary-based waiting. These approaches are evaluated in lifelong simulation over a 3.6km topological map representing a commercial polytunnel environment. Their performances are contrasted against 5 baseline algorithms with varying robotic fleet sizes. The Fragment Planners achieved significant gains in throughput compared with Prioritised Planning (PP) and Priority-Based Search (PBS) algorithms. They further demonstrated a task throughput of 95% of the optimal task throughput over the same time period. This work shows that, for long-term deployment of agricultural robots in corridor-dominant agricultural environments, resource-centric MRPP approaches are a necessity for high-efficacy operational planning.

</details>


### [96] [SldprtNet: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset for CAD Generation in Language-Driven 3D Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13098)
*Ruogu Li,Sikai Li,Yao Mu,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SldprtNet是一个包含24.2万个工业零件的大型多模态数据集，支持语义驱动的CAD建模、几何深度学习以及3D设计多模态模型的训练与微调，提供3D模型、参数化脚本、渲染图像和自然语言描述等多种对齐模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏大规模、多模态对齐的工业零件数据集来支持语义驱动的CAD建模和跨模态学习。现有数据集通常模态单一或规模有限，难以满足工业设计领域对多模态模型训练的需求。

Method: 1) 收集24.2万个工业零件，提供.step和.sldprt格式3D模型；2) 开发支持13种CAD命令的编码器/解码器，实现3D模型与结构化文本表示的无损转换；3) 通过合并7个视角的渲染图像减少输入token长度；4) 使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B多模态模型生成零件外观和功能的自然语言描述；5) 人工验证对齐描述、图像和3D模型。

Result: 构建了包含24.2万个样本的SldprtNet数据集，每个样本包含完全对齐的3D模型文件、参数化建模脚本、渲染图像和自然语言描述。在数据集子集上微调基线模型，验证了图像加文本输入相比纯文本输入的必要性和价值，证实了多模态数据集对CAD生成的重要性。

Conclusion: SldprtNet是一个全面的多模态数据集，具有真实工业零件、支持工具、多样模态和确保的模型复杂度与几何特征多样性，为语义驱动的CAD建模和跨模态学习提供了重要资源，证明了多模态数据集在CAD生成中的必要性。

Abstract: We introduce SldprtNet, a large-scale dataset comprising over 242,000 industrial parts, designed for semantic-driven CAD modeling, geometric deep learning, and the training and fine-tuning of multimodal models for 3D design. The dataset provides 3D models in both .step and .sldprt formats to support diverse training and testing. To enable parametric modeling and facilitate dataset scalability, we developed supporting tools, an encoder and a decoder, which support 13 types of CAD commands and enable lossless transformation between 3D models and a structured text representation. Additionally, each sample is paired with a composite image created by merging seven rendered views from different viewpoints of the 3D model, effectively reducing input token length and accelerating inference. By combining this image with the parameterized text output from the encoder, we employ the lightweight multimodal language model Qwen2.5-VL-7B to generate a natural language description of each part's appearance and functionality. To ensure accuracy, we manually verified and aligned the generated descriptions, rendered images, and 3D models. These descriptions, along with the parameterized modeling scripts, rendered images, and 3D model files, are fully aligned to construct SldprtNet. To assess its effectiveness, we fine-tuned baseline models on a dataset subset, comparing image-plus-text inputs with text-only inputs. Results confirm the necessity and value of multimodal datasets for CAD generation. It features carefully selected real-world industrial parts, supporting tools for scalable dataset expansion, diverse modalities, and ensured diversity in model complexity and geometric features, making it a comprehensive multimodal dataset built for semantic-driven CAD modeling and cross-modal learning.

</details>


### [97] [Evaluating VLMs' Spatial Reasoning Over Robot Motion: A Step Towards Robot Planning with Motion Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13100)
*Wenxi Wu,Jingjing Zhang,Martim Brandão*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估四种最先进的视觉语言模型在机器人运动空间推理方面的能力，使用四种查询方法，发现Qwen2.5-VL在零样本情况下达到71.4%准确率，GPT-4o表现较差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解用户指令和环境中物体空间关系对智能机器人系统至关重要。虽然视觉语言模型具有自然语言和空间推理能力，但尚不清楚其在机器人运动规划中执行用户偏好或约束（如距离、拓扑属性、运动风格）的空间推理能力程度

Method: 评估四种最先进的视觉语言模型在机器人运动空间推理方面的能力，使用四种不同的查询方法。评估两种类型的运动偏好（物体接近度和路径风格），并分析准确性与计算成本（token数量）之间的权衡

Result: 使用最高性能的查询方法，Qwen2.5-VL在零样本情况下达到71.4%准确率，在较小模型上微调后达到75%。GPT-4o导致较低性能。研究还分析了准确性与计算成本之间的权衡

Conclusion: 这项工作显示了视觉语言模型与机器人运动规划管道集成的潜力，但当前模型在空间推理能力方面仍有提升空间

Abstract: Understanding user instructions and object spatial relations in surrounding environments is crucial for intelligent robot systems to assist humans in various tasks. The natural language and spatial reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have the potential to enhance the generalization of robot planners on new tasks, objects, and motion specifications. While foundation models have been applied to task planning, it is still unclear the degree to which they have the capability of spatial reasoning required to enforce user preferences or constraints on motion, such as desired distances from objects, topological properties, or motion style preferences. In this paper, we evaluate the capability of four state-of-the-art VLMs at spatial reasoning over robot motion, using four different querying methods. Our results show that, with the highest-performing querying method, Qwen2.5-VL achieves 71.4% accuracy zero-shot and 75% on a smaller model after fine-tuning, and GPT-4o leads to lower performance. We evaluate two types of motion preferences (object-proximity and path-style), and we also analyze the trade-off between accuracy and computation cost in number of tokens. This work shows some promise in the potential of VLM integration with robot motion planning pipelines.

</details>


### [98] [A Feasibility-Enhanced Control Barrier Function Method for Multi-UAV Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13103)
*Qishen Zhong,Junlong Wu,Jian Yang,Guanwei Xiao,Junqi Wu,Zimeng Jiang,Pingan Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可行性增强控制屏障函数(FECBF)框架，解决多无人机密集场景中CBF-QP可行性问题，通过符号一致性约束缓解内部不兼容性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集多无人机场景中，多个CBF约束之间的内部不兼容性会损害CBF二次规划(CBF-QP)的可行性，导致碰撞避免性能下降

Method: 分析CBF约束的内部兼容性，推导内部兼容性的充分条件，引入符号一致性约束缓解内部不兼容性，使用最坏情况估计和松弛变量构建去中心化CBF-QP

Result: 仿真结果表明，在密集场景中相比现有基线方法，所提方法显著减少不可行性并提高碰撞避免性能；时变延迟下的仿真验证了方法的鲁棒性；真实世界实验验证了实际适用性

Conclusion: 提出的FECBF框架通过符号一致性约束有效解决了多无人机密集场景中CBF约束的内部不兼容性问题，提高了CBF-QP的可行性和碰撞避免性能

Abstract: This paper presents a feasibility-enhanced control barrier function (FECBF) framework for multi-UAV collision avoidance. In dense multi-UAV scenarios, the feasibility of the CBF quadratic program (CBF-QP) can be compromised due to internal incompatibility among multiple CBF constraints. To address this issue, we analyze the internal compatibility of CBF constraints and derive a sufficient condition for internal compatibility. Based on this condition, a sign-consistency constraint is introduced to mitigate internal incompatibility. The proposed constraint is incorporated into a decentralized CBF-QP formulation using worst-case estimates and slack variables. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces infeasibility and improves collision avoidance performance compared with existing baselines in dense scenarios. Additional simulations under varying time delays demonstrate the robustness of the proposed method. Real-world experiments validate the practical applicability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [99] [Panoramic Multimodal Semantic Occupancy Prediction for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13108)
*Guoqiang Zhao,Zhe Yang,Sheng Wu,Fei Teng,Mengfei Duan,Yuanfan Zheng,Kai Luo,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个四足机器人全景多模态占据数据集PanoMMOcc和对应感知框架VoxelHound，通过垂直抖动补偿和多模态提示融合提升复杂环境下的占据预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占据预测方法主要针对轮式自动驾驶设计，严重依赖RGB线索，在复杂环境中鲁棒性不足。四足机器人的全景视觉和腿部运动带来独特挑战，需要专门的数据集和算法

Method: 1) 创建PanoMMOcc数据集，包含四种传感模态的多样化场景；2) 提出VoxelHound框架，包含垂直抖动补偿模块(VJC)缓解俯仰滚转扰动，以及多模态信息提示融合模块(MIPF)联合利用全景视觉和辅助模态；3) 建立基准测试和详细数据分析

Result: VoxelHound在PanoMMOcc数据集上达到最先进性能，mIoU提升4.16%。数据集和代码将公开，包含校准工具

Conclusion: 该工作填补了四足机器人全景多模态占据感知的空白，提出的数据集和框架为具身机器人系统的3D感知研究提供了重要基础，能够提升复杂环境下的鲁棒性

Abstract: Panoramic imagery provides holistic 360° visual coverage for perception in quadruped robots. However, existing occupancy prediction methods are mainly designed for wheeled autonomous driving and rely heavily on RGB cues, limiting their robustness in complex environments. To bridge this gap, (1) we present PanoMMOcc, the first real-world panoramic multimodal occupancy dataset for quadruped robots, featuring four sensing modalities across diverse scenes. (2) We propose a panoramic multimodal occupancy perception framework, VoxelHound, tailored for legged mobility and spherical imaging. Specifically, we design (i) a Vertical Jitter Compensation (VJC) module to mitigate severe viewpoint perturbations caused by body pitch and roll during mobility, enabling more consistent spatial reasoning, and (ii) an effective Multimodal Information Prompt Fusion (MIPF) module that jointly leverages panoramic visual cues and auxiliary modalities to enhance volumetric occupancy prediction. (3) We establish a benchmark based on PanoMMOcc and provide detailed data analysis to enable systematic evaluation of perception methods under challenging embodied scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VoxelHound achieves state-of-the-art performance on PanoMMOcc (+4.16%} in mIoU). The dataset and code will be publicly released to facilitate future research on panoramic multimodal 3D perception for embodied robotic systems at https://github.com/SXDR/PanoMMOcc, along with the calibration tools released at https://github.com/losehu/CameraLiDAR-Calib.

</details>


### [100] [DecoVLN: Decoupling Observation, Reasoning, and Correction for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13133)
*Zihao Xin,Wentong Li,Yixuan Jiang,Bin Wang,Runming Cong,Jie Qin,Shengjun Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeCoVLN：面向长时程视觉语言导航的流式感知与闭环控制框架，通过自适应记忆优化和状态-动作对级纠错微调解决长期记忆构建和误差累积问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法面临两大挑战：1）构建有效的长期记忆库；2）克服误差累积问题。这些问题限制了智能体在复杂3D环境中执行长时程指令导航的能力

Method: 提出DeCoVLN框架：1）将长期记忆构建建模为优化问题，引入自适应精炼机制，通过迭代优化统一评分函数从历史候选池中选择关键帧；2）引入状态-动作对级纠错微调策略，利用状态间的测地距离量化偏离专家轨迹的程度，在可信区域内收集高质量数据并过滤低相关性污染数据

Result: 大量实验证明了DeCoVLN的有效性，已在真实世界环境中部署应用

Conclusion: DeCoVLN通过自适应记忆优化和纠错微调策略，有效解决了视觉语言导航中的长期记忆构建和误差累积问题，实现了鲁棒的流式感知和闭环控制

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to follow long-horizon instructions and navigate complex 3D environments. However, existing approaches face two major challenges: constructing an effective long-term memory bank and overcoming the compounding errors problem. To address these issues, we propose DecoVLN, an effective framework designed for robust streaming perception and closed-loop control in long-horizon navigation. First, we formulate long-term memory construction as an optimization problem and introduce adaptive refinement mechanism that selects frames from a historical candidate pool by iteratively optimizing a unified scoring function. This function jointly balances three key criteria: semantic relevance to the instruction, visual diversity from the selected memory, and temporal coverage of the historical trajectory. Second, to alleviate compounding errors, we introduce a state-action pair-level corrective finetuning strategy. By leveraging geodesic distance between states to precisely quantify deviation from the expert trajectory, the agent collects high-quality state-action pairs in the trusted region while filtering out the polluted data with low relevance. This improves both the efficiency and stability of error correction. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of DecoVLN, and we have deployed it in real-world environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [RadEar: A Self-Supervised RF Backscatter System for Voice Eavesdropping and Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12446)
*Qijun Wang,Peihao Yan,Chunqi Qian,Huacheng Zeng*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: RadEar是一个基于RF反向散射的隐蔽语音窃听系统，通过墙壁实现语音窃听，包含无电池标签和外部RF读取器，采用双谐振器设计和自监督学习进行语音分离与降噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音窃听对个人隐私和信息安全构成日益增长的威胁，需要开发能够穿透墙壁的隐蔽窃听技术。

Method: 系统包含两个组件：1) 部署在目标空间内的无电池RF反向散射标签，采用紧凑的双谐振器设计实现能量高效的频率调制，通过分离激励和反射频率来减轻自干扰；2) 位于室外的RF读取器，执行信号解调、语音分离和降噪，采用基于混音目标的自监督学习模型，无需真实标签。

Result: 在真实场景中制造和评估RadEar，证明其能够在实际约束条件下以高保真度恢复和分离人类语音。

Conclusion: RadEar展示了通过墙壁进行隐蔽语音窃听的可行性，采用创新的RF反向散射架构和自监督学习方法，在隐私和安全方面具有重要影响。

Abstract: Eavesdropping on voice conversations presents a growing threat to personal privacy and information security. In this paper, we present RadEar, a novel RF backscatter-based system designed to enable covert voice eavesdropping through walls. RadEar consists of two key components: (i) a batteryless RF backscatter tag covertly deployed inside the target space, and (ii) an RF reader located outside the room that performs signal demodulation, voice separation, and denoising. The tag features a compact, dual-resonator design that achieves energy-efficient frequency modulation for continuous voice eavesdropping while mitigating self-interference by separating excitation and reflection frequencies. To overcome the challenges of weak signal reception and overlapping speech, the RF reader employs self-supervised learning models for voice separation and denoising, trained using a remix-based objective without requiring ground-truth labels. We fabricate and evaluate RadEar in real-world scenarios, demonstrating its ability to recover and separate human speech with high fidelity under practical constraints.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Bridging the Gap Between Security Metrics and Key Risk Indicators: An Empirical Framework for Vulnerability Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12450)
*Emad Sherif,Iryna Yevseyeva,Vitor Basto-Fernandes,Allan Cook*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于预期损失分解的复合关键风险指标(KRI)，用于改进漏洞修复优先级排序，相比传统CVSS评分在真实漏洞利用数据上表现显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织普遍依赖CVSS严重性评分来优先处理漏洞修复，但CVSS在真实漏洞利用数据上的预测性能接近随机水平(AUPRC 0.011)，无法有效识别实际被利用的漏洞，需要更准确的风险评估方法。

Method: 提出基于预期损失分解的复合关键风险指标(KRI)，整合威胁、影响和暴露三个维度。使用包含280,694个CVE的综合数据集，在已知被利用漏洞(KEV)目录上评估KRI框架，并与CVSS和EPSS进行对比分析。

Result: KRI在ROC-AUC上达到0.927，AUPRC为0.223，显著优于CVSS的0.747和0.011。虽然EPSS在AUPRC(0.365)上高于KRI，但KRI在影响加权修复价值捕获方面表现更好：在k=500时捕获92.3%的价值(EPSS为82.6%)，并多发现1.75倍的关键严重性被利用CVE。当严重性溢价超过2时，KRI的净效益超过EPSS。

Conclusion: EPSS是漏洞利用检测的稳健基线，而KRI框架是组织寻求将修复工作与切实风险降低对齐的更好选择。KRI通过重新排序考虑影响和暴露，能更有效地指导资源分配以实现最大风险降低。

Abstract: Organisations overwhelmingly prioritize vulnerability remediation using Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS) severity scores, yet CVSS classifiers achieve an Area Under the Precision-Recall Curve (AUPRC) of 0.011 on real-world exploitation data, near random chance. We propose a composite Key Risk Indicator grounded in expected-loss decomposition, integrating dimensions of threat, impact, and exposure. We evaluated the KRI framework against the Known Exploited Vulnerabilities (KEV) catalog using a comprehensive dataset of 280,694 Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVEs). KRI achieves Receiver Operating Characteristic Area Under the Curve (ROC-AUC) 0.927 and AUPRC 0.223 versus 0.747 and 0.011 for CVSS (24 percents, 20). Ablation analysis shows Exploit Prediction Scoring System (EPSS) alone achieves AUPRC 0.365, higher than full KRI (0.223), confirming that EPSS and KRI serve distinct objectives: EPSS maximizes raw exploit detection, while KRI re-orders by impact and exposure, capturing 92.3 percents of impact-weighted remediation value at k=500 versus 82.6 percents for EPSS, and surfacing 1.75 more Critical-severity exploited CVEs. KRI's net benefit exceeds EPSS whenever the severity premium exceeds 2. While EPSS serves as a robust baseline for exploit detection, the KRI framework is the superior choice for organizations seeking to align remediation efforts with tangible risk reduction.

</details>


### [103] [Hunting CUDA Bugs at Scale with cuFuzz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12485)
*Mohamed Tarek Ibn ziad,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: cuFuzz是首个实用的CUDA模糊测试工具，通过解决内核级模糊测试假阳性、缺乏设备端覆盖率引导反馈以及覆盖率与消毒工具不兼容三大障碍，显著提升GPU程序测试效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU在现代软件中日益重要，但其异构主机-设备执行模型和不断扩展的软件栈使得GPU程序容易产生内存安全和并发错误，这些错误难以通过静态分析发现。虽然模糊测试结合动态错误检查工具提供了可行的解决方案，但在GPU领域仍未被充分利用。

Method: cuFuzz采用全程序模糊测试避免独立测试设备端内核导致的假阳性；利用NVBit工具对设备端指令进行插桩，并将结果覆盖率与基于编译器的主机端覆盖率合并；通过在不同进程中执行主机端和设备端消毒器，将消毒与覆盖率收集解耦。

Result: cuFuzz在14个CUDA程序中发现了43个先前未知的错误（其中19个在商业库中），包括非法内存访问、未初始化读取和数据竞争。与基线方法相比，cuFuzz发现了显著更多的边覆盖和唯一输入，特别是在闭源目标上。研究还量化了不同组件的执行时间开销，并增加了持久模式支持以提高整体模糊测试吞吐量。

Conclusion: cuFuzz通过解决GPU模糊测试的关键障碍，成为GPU测试工具箱中一个有效且可部署的补充工具，显著提升了GPU程序的安全性和可靠性测试能力。

Abstract: GPUs play an increasingly important role in modern software. However, the heterogeneous host-device execution model and expanding software stacks make GPU programs prone to memory-safety and concurrency bugs that evade static analysis. While fuzz-testing, combined with dynamic error checking tools, offers a plausible solution, it remains underutilized for GPUs. In this work, we identify three main obstacles limiting prior GPU fuzzing efforts: (1) kernel-level fuzzing leading to false positives, (2) lack of device-side coverage-guided feedback, and (3) incompatibility between coverage and sanitization tools. We present cuFuzz, the first CUDA-oriented fuzzer that makes GPU fuzzing practical by addressing these obstacles.
  cuFuzz uses whole program fuzzing to avoid false positives from independently fuzzing device-side kernels. It leverages NVBit to instrument device-side instructions and merges the resultant coverage with compiler-based host coverage. Finally, cuFuzz decouples sanitization from coverage collection by executing host- and device-side sanitizers in separate processes. cuFuzz uncovers 43 previously unknown bugs (19 in commercial libraries) across 14 CUDA programs, including illegal memory accesses, uninitialized reads, and data races. cuFuzz achieves significantly more discovered edges and unique inputs compared to baseline approaches, especially on closed-source targets. Moreover, we quantify the execution time overheads of the different cuFuzz components and add persistent-mode support to improve the overall fuzzing throughput. Our results demonstrate that cuFuzz is an effective and deployable addition to the GPU testing toolbox. cuFuzz is publicly available at https://github.com/NVlabs/cuFuzz/.

</details>


### [104] [Verification of Robust Properties for Access Control Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13181)
*Alexander V. Gheorghiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出"鲁棒属性验证"概念，用于验证访问控制策略的结构性承诺，无论待定决策如何解决或后续扩展如何变化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有访问控制策略验证方法要求策略完整且完全确定，但实践中策略是迭代开发、由独立维护组件组合而成，并随着组织结构演化而扩展

Method: 引入鲁棒属性验证问题，定义支持判断$\Vdash_{P}φ$，证明其组合性（单调性定理），并将该判断简化为二阶逻辑编程语言中的证明搜索

Result: 建立了该简化的可靠性和完备性，得到了鲁棒安全属性的有限且可执行的验证过程

Conclusion: 鲁棒属性验证为访问控制策略的迭代开发和扩展提供了实用的验证框架，解决了传统方法在策略不完整情况下的局限性

Abstract: Existing methods for verifying access control policies require the policy to be complete and fully determined before verification can proceed, but in practice policies are developed iteratively, composed from independently maintained components, and extended as organisational structures evolve. We introduce robust property verification: the problem of determining what a policy's structure commits it to regardless of how pending decisions are resolved and regardless of subsequent extension. We define a support judgment $\Vdash_{P}φ$ stating that policy $P$ has robust property $φ$, with connectives for implication, conjunction, disjunction, and negation, prove that it is compositional (verified properties persist under policy extension by a monotonicity theorem), and show that despite quantifying universally over all possible policy extensions the judgment reduces to proof search in a second-order logic programming language. Soundness and completeness of this reduction are established, yielding a finitary and executable verification procedure for robust security properties.

</details>


### [105] [Keys on Doormats: Exposed API Credentials on the Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12498)
*Nurullah Demir,Yash Vekaria,Georgios Smaragdakis,Zakir Durumeric*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统分析了API凭证在网页上的暴露情况，通过分析1000万个网页发现大量敏感凭证被公开暴露，包括全球银行和固件开发商的网站，并揭示了JavaScript环境是主要暴露源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API密钥和令牌等敏感凭证的安全处理至关重要，先前研究主要关注云平台和GitHub等环境的凭证暴露，但网页环境尚未被系统研究。网页作为广泛使用的平台，其上的凭证暴露可能带来严重安全风险。

Method: 研究分析了1000万个网页，识别和提取API凭证。通过分析存档数据评估凭证暴露的持续时间，并识别网页特有的暴露向量和根本原因。最后进行了负责任的披露工作。

Result: 在近10,000个网页中发现了来自14个服务提供商（如云和支付提供商）的1,748个不同凭证。凭证暴露时间从一个月到数年不等。大多数暴露源自JavaScript环境，负责任的披露显著减少了网页上的凭证暴露。

Conclusion: API凭证在网页上广泛且公开暴露，JavaScript环境是主要暴露源。负责任的披露是有效的缓解措施，需要开发者和组织提高对网页凭证暴露风险的认识并采取适当防护措施。

Abstract: Application programming interfaces (APIs) have become a central part of the modern IT environment, allowing developers to enrich the functionality of applications and interact with third parties such as cloud and payment providers. This interaction often occurs through authentication mechanisms that rely on sensitive credentials such as API keys and tokens that require secure handling. Exposure of these credentials can pose significant consequences to organizations, as malicious attackers can gain access to related services. Previous studies have shown exposure of these sensitive credentials in different environments such as cloud platforms and GitHub. However, the web remains unexplored.
  In this paper, we study exposure of credentials on the web by analyzing 10M webpages. Our findings reveal that API credentials are widely and publicly exposed on the web, including highly popular and critical webpages such as those of global banks and firmware developers. We identify 1,748 distinct credentials from 14 service providers (e.g., cloud and payment providers) across nearly 10,000 webpages. Moreover, our analysis of archived data suggest credentials to remain exposed for periods ranging from a month to several years. We characterize web-specific exposure vectors and root causes, finding that most originate from JavaScript environments. We also discuss the outcomes of our responsible disclosure efforts that demonstrated a substantial reduction in credential exposure on the web.

</details>


### [106] [Colluding LoRA: A Composite Attack on LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12681)
*Sihao Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CoLoRA攻击利用LoRA适配器的线性组合实现安全绕过，单个适配器看似良性，但组合后导致模型对齐退化，无需对抗性提示即可响应有害请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模块化LLM供应链防御系统存在组合盲区，仅对单个模块进行验证，无法检测多个适配器组合时可能产生的安全风险，攻击者可利用这一漏洞绕过安全机制。

Method: CoLoRA攻击通过设计多个看似良性的LoRA适配器，这些适配器单独加载时表现正常，但当特定组合被加载时，其线性组合会系统性地破坏模型的安全对齐，实现广泛的拒绝抑制。

Result: 在多个开源权重LLM上，CoLoRA攻击中单个适配器表现出良性行为，但组合后达到高攻击成功率，表明当前防御系统无法有效检测组合攻击。

Conclusion: 保护模块化LLM供应链需要超越单模块验证，转向组合感知的防御机制，因为组合攻击利用了当前防御系统的计算不可行性漏洞。

Abstract: We introduce Colluding LoRA (CoLoRA), an attack in which each adapter appears benign and plausibly functional in isolation, yet their linear composition consistently compromises safety. Unlike attacks that depend on specific input triggers or prompt patterns, CoLoRA is a composition-triggered broad refusal suppression: once a particular set of adapters is loaded, the model undergoes effective alignment degradation, complying with harmful requests without requiring adversarial prompts or suffixes. This attack exploits the combinatorial blindness of current defense systems, where exhaustively scanning all compositions is computationally intractable. Across several open-weight LLMs, CoLoRA achieves benign behavior individually yet high attack success rate after composition, indicating that securing modular LLM supply-chains requires moving beyond single-module verification toward composition-aware defenses.

</details>


### [107] [Almost-Free Queue Jumping for Prior Inputs in Private Neural Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12946)
*Qiao Zhang,Minghui Xu,Tingchuang Zhang,Xiuzhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PrivQJ框架在隐私保护机器学习即服务中实现高效优先级处理，通过槽位回收技术让紧急请求搭便车，相比现有系统降低一个数量级的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合原语框架虽然提升了推理效率，但批量处理输入时只能顺序执行，缺乏对紧急请求的优先级处理能力。简单的插队方法会带来显著的计算和通信开销，增加队列中其他输入的延迟。

Method: 提出PrivQJ框架，采用处理中槽位回收技术，利用输入间的共享计算，让优先级输入能够搭便车到正在进行的批量计算中，几乎不增加额外的密码学成本。

Result: 理论分析和实验结果表明，相比最先进的PP-MLaaS系统，PrivQJ能够降低超过一个数量级的开销。

Conclusion: PrivQJ首次在隐私保护批量推理中实现了高效的队列跳转，在不降低整体系统性能的前提下支持优先级处理，为实时隐私保护机器学习服务提供了重要改进。

Abstract: Privacy-Preserving Machine Learning as a Service (PP-MLaaS) enables secure neural network inference by integrating cryptographic primitives such as homomorphic encryption (HE) and multi-party computation (MPC), protecting both client data and server models. Recent mixed-primitive frameworks have significantly improved inference efficiency, yet they process batched inputs sequentially, offering little flexibility for prioritizing urgent requests. Naïve queue jumping introduces considerable computational and communication overhead, increasing non-negligible latency for in-queue inputs.
  We initiate the study of privacy-preserving queue jumping in batched inference and propose PrivQJ, a novel framework that enables efficient priority handling without degrading overall system performance. PrivQJ exploits shared computation across inputs via in-processing slot recycling, allowing prior inputs to be piggybacked onto ongoing batch computation with almost no additional cryptographic cost. Both theoretical analysis and experimental results demonstrate over an order-of-magnitude reduction in overhead compared to state-of-the-art PP-MLaaS systems.

</details>


### [108] [Mitigating Collusion in Proofs of Liabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12990)
*Malcom Mohamed,Ghassan Karame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的许可证明负债模型，通过无需用户合作即可检测不诚实服务商行为，解决了现有方案在服务商与用户共谋攻击场景下的安全缺陷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加密货币交易所使用的负债证明方案存在设计缺陷，无法抵抗服务商与现有用户共谋的现实攻击场景，需要更安全的方案来增强用户信任

Method: 提出许可证明负债模型，核心是新的许可向量承诺原语，结合KZG承诺的同态特性和BLS签名，确保承诺向量仅包含用户明确签名的值

Result: 原型实现表明，在提供更强安全性的同时，服务器性能相比现有方案提升高达10倍

Conclusion: 许可证明负债模型解决了现有负债证明方案的安全漏洞，通过创新的许可向量承诺原语实现了更强的安全性和更好的性能

Abstract: Cryptocurrency exchanges use proofs of liabilities (PoLs) to prove to their customers their liabilities committed on-chain, thereby enhancing their trust in the service. Unfortunately, a close examination of currently deployed and academic PoLs reveals significant shortcomings in their designs. For instance, existing schemes cannot resist realistic attack scenarios in which the provider colludes with an existing user. In this paper, we propose a new model, dubbed permissioned PoL, that addresses this gap by not requiring cooperation from users to detect a dishonest provider's potential misbehavior. At the core of our proposal lies a novel primitive, which we call Permissioned Vector Commitment (PVC), to ensure that a committed vector only contains values that users have explicitly signed. We provide an efficient PVC and PoL construction that carefully combines homomorphic properties of KZG commitments and BLS-based signatures. Our prototype implementation shows that, despite the stronger security, our proposal also improves server performance (by up to $10\times$) compared to prior PoLs.

</details>


### [109] [FraudFox: Adaptable Fraud Detection in the Real World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13014)
*Matthew Butler,Yi Fan,Christos Faloutsos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FraudFox是面向资源受限环境下对抗攻击的欺诈检测系统，通过动态权重分配和最优决策面解决风险评估模块融合与交易筛选问题，已在亚马逊生产环境中部署并取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的对抗环境中，需要解决两个核心问题：1) 如何将多个风险评估模块（oracles）的风险评分有效融合；2) 在历史数据、业务目标和限制条件下，如何智能决定哪些交易应通过、哪些应送交人工调查。欺诈者会根据系统过往决策调整行为，因此系统必须具备适应性。

Method: 针对第一个问题（oracle权重分配），使用扩展卡尔曼滤波器配合动态重要性权重，自动持续更新各风险评估模块的权重。针对第二个问题（交易筛选），推导最优决策面并计算帕累托最优集，支持假设分析。系统强调适应性，能够应对欺诈者行为变化。

Result: FraudFox系统具备可扩展性、适应性、高效性，已在亚马逊生产环境中部署。该系统增强了欺诈预防子系统，并带来了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: FraudFox为资源受限环境下的对抗性欺诈检测提供了有效解决方案，通过动态权重分配和最优决策面技术，在满足业务约束的同时实现了高效的交易筛选，并能适应欺诈者行为的变化。

Abstract: The proposed method (FraudFox) provides solutions to adversarial attacks in a resource constrained environment. We focus on questions like the following: How suspicious is `Smith', trying to buy \$500 shoes, on Monday 3am? How to merge the risk scores, from a handful of risk-assessment modules (`oracles') in an adversarial environment? More importantly, given historical data (orders, prices, and what-happened afterwards), and business goals/restrictions, which transactions, like the `Smith' transaction above, which ones should we `pass', versus send to human investigators? The business restrictions could be: `at most $x$ investigations are feasible', or `at most \$$y$ lost due to fraud'. These are the two research problems we focus on, in this work. One approach to address the first problem (`oracle-weighting'), is by using Extended Kalman Filters with dynamic importance weights, to automatically and continuously update our weights for each 'oracle'. For the second problem, we show how to derive an optimal decision surface, and how to compute the Pareto optimal set, to allow what-if questions. An important consideration is adaptation: Fraudsters will change their behavior, according to our past decisions; thus, we need to adapt accordingly. The resulting system, \method, is scalable, adaptable to changing fraudster behavior, effective, and already in \textbf{production} at Amazon. FraudFox augments a fraud prevention sub-system and has led to significant performance gains.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [GPU-Accelerated Genetic Programming for Symbolic Regression with Beagle Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12292)
*Nathan Haut,Ilya Basin,Marzieh Kianinejad,Ruchika Gupta,Elijah Smith,Zachary Perrico,Wolfgang Banzhaf*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: Beagle是一个新的GPU加速遗传编程框架，用于符号回归任务，在Feynman数据集上显著优于CPU系统StackGP和PySR


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个能够在GPU上高效执行遗传编程任务的软件框架，以利用GPU的并行计算能力加速符号回归过程，解决现有CPU系统计算速度慢的问题

Method: Beagle框架采用GPU并行处理种群个体和训练样本，最大化GPU平台的吞吐量。研究中使用两种适应度函数：点对点误差函数和相关性适应度函数，并在Feynman符号回归数据集上进行基准测试

Result: 在相同的时钟预算下，Beagle的GPU加速符号回归显著优于领先的CPU框架StackGP和PySR。GPU并行计算大幅提升了符号回归任务的执行效率

Conclusion: GPU加速的遗传编程框架Beagle在符号回归任务中表现出卓越性能，为遗传编程的高效执行提供了新的解决方案，证明了GPU并行计算在进化计算领域的巨大潜力

Abstract: Beagle is a new software framework that enables execution of Genetic Programming tasks on the GPU. Currently available for symbolic regression, it processes individuals of the population and fitness cases for training in a way that maximizes throughput on extant GPU platforms. In this contribution, we report on the benchmarking of Beagle on the Feynman Symbolic Regression dataset and compare its performance with a fast CPU system called StackGP and the widely available PySR system under the same wall clock budget. We also report on the use of two different fitness functions, one a point-to-point error function, the other a correlation fitness function. The results demonstrate that the Beagle's GPU-aided Symbolic Regression significantly outperforms leading CPU-based frameworks.

</details>


### [111] [SRAM-Based Compute-in-Memory Accelerator for Linear-decay Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12739)
*Hongyang Shang,Shuai Dong,Yahan Yang,Junyi Yang,Peng Zhou,Arindam Basu*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出基于SRAM的存内计算架构，通过线性衰减近似和并行更新机制，解决SNN中神经元状态更新的串行瓶颈，显著提升能效和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN的吞吐量受限于神经元膜电位的串行更新，虽然许多硬件加速器和存内计算架构已高效并行化突触操作（W×I），但后续的状态更新仍需O(N)时间刷新所有神经元膜电位，这导致状态更新成为SNN推理中的主要延迟和能耗瓶颈。

Method: 1. 算法层面：用线性衰减近似替代传统的指数膜衰减，将昂贵的乘法运算转换为简单加法，精度损失仅约1%。2. 架构层面：引入存内并行更新方案，在SRAM阵列内直接执行原位衰减，消除全局顺序更新的需求。

Result: 在基准SNN工作负载上评估，所提方法实现了1.1倍到16.7倍的SOP能耗降低，同时提供15.9倍到69倍的能效提升，相对于原始衰减模型的精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 这项工作强调，除了加速（W×I）计算外，在存内计算架构中优化状态更新动态对于实现可扩展、低功耗和实时的神经形态处理至关重要。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have emerged as a biologically inspired alternative to conventional deep networks, offering event-driven and energy-efficient computation. However, their throughput remains constrained by the serial update of neuron membrane states. While many hardware accelerators and Compute-in-Memory (CIM) architectures efficiently parallelize the synaptic operation (W x I) achieving O(1) complexity for matrix-vector multiplication, the subsequent state update step still requires O(N) time to refresh all neuron membrane potentials. This mismatch makes state update the dominant latency and energy bottleneck in SNN inference. To address this challenge, we propose an SRAM-based CIM for SNN with Linear Decay Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LD-LIF) Neuron that co-optimizes algorithm and hardware. At the algorithmic level, we replace the conventional exponential membrane decay with a linear decay approximation, converting costly multiplications into simple additions while accuracy drops only around 1%. At the architectural level, we introduce an in-memory parallel update scheme that performs in-place decay directly within the SRAM array, eliminating the need for global sequential updates. Evaluated on benchmark SNN workloads, the proposed method achieves a 1.1 x to 16.7 x reduction of SOP energy consumption, while providing 15.9 x to 69 x more energy efficiency, with negligible accuracy loss relative to original decay models. This work highlights that beyond accelerating the (W x I) computation, optimizing state-update dynamics within CIM architectures is essential for scalable, low-power, and real-time neuromorphic processing.

</details>


### [112] [Federated Few-Shot Learning on Neuromorphic Hardware: An Empirical Study Across Physical Edge Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13037)
*Steven Motta,Gioele Nanni*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该研究首次在神经形态硬件上实现联邦学习，测试了四种权重交换策略，发现神经元级拼接（FedUnion）能保持精度，而元素级平均（FedAvg）会破坏精度。特征质量是主要影响因素，特征维度从64扩展到256可获得77.0%的联邦准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态硬件上的联邦学习尚未探索，因为片上STDP产生的是二进制权重更新，而非标准算法假设的浮点梯度。需要研究如何在神经形态硬件上实现有效的联邦学习。

Method: 使用BrainChip Akida AKD1000处理器构建双节点联邦系统，进行约1580次实验，测试四种权重交换策略。分析特征维度扩展（64到256）的影响，研究特征质量对准确率的影响机制。

Result: 神经元级拼接（FedUnion）能保持精度，元素级平均（FedAvg）会破坏精度（p=0.002）。特征维度从64扩展到256可获得77.0%的最佳策略联邦准确率（p<0.001）。特征质量是主要影响因素，域自适应微调上游特征提取器贡献了大部分准确率提升。

Conclusion: 神经形态硬件上的联邦学习是可行的，但需要专门的权重交换策略。特征质量是决定联邦学习效果的关键因素，神经元原型互补性机制解释了跨节点传输的效果差异。

Abstract: Federated learning on neuromorphic hardware remains unexplored because on-chip spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP) produces binary weight updates rather than the floating-point gradients assumed by standard algorithms. We build a two-node federated system with BrainChip Akida AKD1000 processors and run approximately 1,580 experimental trials across seven analysis phases. Of four weight-exchange strategies tested, neuron-level concatenation (FedUnion) consistently preserves accuracy while element-wise weight averaging (FedAvg) destroys it (p = 0.002). Domain-adaptive fine-tuning of the upstream feature extractor accounts for most of the accuracy gains, confirming feature quality as the dominant factor. Scaling feature dimensionality from 64 to 256 yields 77.0% best-strategy federated accuracy (n=30, p < 0.001). Two independent asymmetries (wider features help federation more than individual learning, while binarization hurts federation more) point to a shared prototype complementarity mechanism: cross-node transfer scales with the distinctiveness of neuron prototypes.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [DIALECTIC: A Multi-Agent System for Startup Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12274)
*Jae Yoon Bae,Simon Malberg,Joyce Galang,Andre Retterath,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: DIALECTIC是一个基于LLM的多智能体系统，用于初创公司评估，通过模拟辩论生成投资决策分数，帮助风险投资者筛选机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险投资者面临大量投资机会但只能投资少数，早期筛选受限于投资者带宽，需要在评估严谨性和评估机会数量之间做出权衡。

Method: 系统首先收集初创公司事实知识并组织成层次化问题树，然后将事实合成为支持和反对投资的自然语言论点，通过模拟辩论迭代批判和精炼论点，最终生成数值决策分数。

Result: 在五个VC基金的真实投资机会上进行回溯测试，显示DIALECTIC在预测初创公司成功方面的准确性与人类VC相当。

Conclusion: DIALECTIC系统能够缓解风险投资者在评估严谨性和机会数量之间的权衡问题，提供有效的投资机会筛选工具。

Abstract: Venture capital (VC) investors face a large number of investment opportunities but only invest in few of these, with even fewer ending up successful. Early-stage screening of opportunities is often limited by investor bandwidth, demanding tradeoffs between evaluation diligence and number of opportunities assessed. To ease this tradeoff, we introduce DIALECTIC, an LLM-based multi-agent system for startup evaluation. DIALECTIC first gathers factual knowledge about a startup and organizes these facts into a hierarchical question tree. It then synthesizes the facts into natural-language arguments for and against an investment and iteratively critiques and refines these arguments through a simulated debate, which surfaces only the most convincing arguments. Our system also produces numeric decision scores that allow investors to rank and thus efficiently prioritize opportunities. We evaluate DIALECTIC through backtesting on real investment opportunities aggregated from five VC funds, showing that DIALECTIC matches the precision of human VCs in predicting startup success.

</details>


### [114] [Collaborative Multi-Agent Optimization for Personalized Memory System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12631)
*Wenyu Mao,Haoyang Liu,Zhao Liu,Haosong Tan,Yaorui Shi,Jiancan Wu,An Zhang,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CoMAM：一个用于多智能体记忆系统的协作强化学习框架，通过联合优化局部智能体来促进协作，解决现有方法忽视跨智能体协作的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统通过多个智能体处理多粒度记忆构建和个性化记忆检索任务，但通常独立优化每个智能体的局部任务（通过提示工程或微调），忽视了跨智能体协作，导致局部优化难以保证全局系统性能

Method: 提出协作强化学习框架CoMAM：1）将智能体执行建模为序列马尔可夫决策过程，嵌入智能体间依赖关系；2）设计局部任务奖励（如记忆构建的信息覆盖率）和全局奖励（查询-答案准确性）；3）通过组级排序一致性量化每个智能体贡献，作为自适应权重分配全局信用并整合局部-全局奖励；4）每个智能体通过整合后的奖励进行优化，使局部改进与全局性能对齐

Result: 实验表明CoMAM优于领先的记忆系统，验证了所提出的协作强化学习框架在联合优化中的有效性

Conclusion: CoMAM通过协作强化学习框架成功解决了多智能体记忆系统中跨智能体协作的优化问题，实现了局部智能体优化与全局系统性能的对齐，为个性化LLM记忆系统提供了有效的联合优化方案

Abstract: Memory systems are crucial to personalized LLMs by mitigating the context window limitation in capturing long-term user-LLM conversations. Typically, such systems leverage multiple agents to handle multi-granular memory construction and personalized memory retrieval tasks. To optimize the system, existing methods focus on specializing agents on their local tasks independently via prompt engineering or fine-tuning. However, they overlook cross-agent collaboration, where independent optimization on local agents hardly guarantees the global system performance. To address this issue, we propose a Collaborative Reinforcement Learning Framework for Multi-Agent Memory Systems (CoMAM), jointly optimizing local agents to facilitate collaboration. Specifically, we regularize agents' execution as a sequential Markov decision process (MDP) to embed inter-agent dependencies into the state transition, yielding both local task rewards (e.g., information coverage for memory construction) and global rewards (i.e., query-answer accuracy). Then, we quantify each agent's contribution via group-level ranking consistency between local and global rewards, treating them as adaptive weights to assign global credit and integrate local-global rewards. Each agent is optimized by these integrated rewards, aligning local improvements with the global performance. Experiments show CoMAM outperforms leading memory systems, validating the efficacy of our proposed collaborative reinforcement learning for joint optimization.

</details>


### [115] [A Generative Model of Conspicuous Consumption and Status Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13220)
*Logan Cross,Jordi Grau-Moya,William A. Cunningham,Alexander Sasha Vezhnevets,Joel Z. Leibo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于适当性理论的计算状态理论，通过LLM智能体模拟验证社会观察和预测模式完成如何内生产生地位符号，展示了功能需求如何转化为地位寻求行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的成本信号理论将偏好视为固定，难以解释符号意义如何随情境变化或动态漂移。需要理解特定商品、信号或行为如何获得声望的生成机制，以及地位符号如何从社会互动中内生产生。

Method: 在Concordia框架中使用基于大型语言模型(LLM)的智能体进行模拟，通过实验操纵社会可见性，观察自然日常例程中的社会互动。研究真实世界奢侈品和程序生成的合成商品，并探索"影响者"智能体如何通过针对性制裁驱动亚文化的形成。

Result: 社会互动将功能需求转化为地位寻求行为；观察到价格飙升和正价格弹性(凡勃伦效应)，排除了预训练偏差作为唯一驱动因素；"影响者"智能体能够通过针对性制裁内生产生不同的亚文化；类似的社会影响效应也推广到非货币信号行为。

Conclusion: 该研究为微观认知和宏观经济社会现象之间提供了生成性桥梁，提供了一种预测文化惯例如何从互动中产生的新方法论。基于适当性理论的计算状态理论能够解释地位符号如何通过社会观察和预测模式完成的反馈循环内生产生。

Abstract: Status signaling drives human behavior and the allocation of scarce resources such as mating opportunities, yet the generative mechanisms governing how specific goods, signals, or behaviors acquire prestige remain a puzzle. Classical frameworks, such as Costly Signaling Theory, treat preferences as fixed and struggle to explain how semiotic meaning changes based on context or drifts dynamically over time, occasionally reaching tipping points. In this work, we propose a computational theory of status grounded in the theory of appropriateness, positing that status symbols emerge endogenously through a feedback loop of social observation and predictive pattern completion. We validate this theory using simulations of groups of Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents in the Concordia framework. By experimentally manipulating social visibility within naturalistic agent daily routines, we demonstrate that social interactions transform functional demand into status-seeking behavior. We observe the emergence of price run-ups and positive price elasticity (Veblen effects) for both real-world luxury items and procedurally generated synthetic goods, ruling out pretraining bias as the sole driver. Furthermore, we demonstrate that "influencer" agents can drive the endogenous formation of distinct subcultures through targeted sanctioning, and find that similar social influence effects generalize to non-monetary signaling behaviors. This work provides a generative bridge between micro-level cognition and macro-level economic and sociological phenomena, offering a new methodology for forecasting how cultural conventions emerge from interaction.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [A Physics-Based Digital Human Twin for Galvanic-Coupling Wearable Communication Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12899)
*Silvia Mura,Chiara Cavigliano,Anna Marcucci,Pietro Savazzi,Anna Vizziello,Maurizio Magarini*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文系统表征了可穿戴式体电耦合通道在窄带和宽带操作下的特性，通过物理一致的数字人体孪生模型将解剖特性、传播几何和电极-皮肤界面映射为可直接用于通信分析的复传递函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可穿戴式体电耦合通信系统的信道特性，建立组织电磁特性与波形失真之间的定量联系，为带宽、接口设计和收发器复杂度之间的权衡提供依据。

Method: 采用物理一致的数字人体孪生模型，将解剖特性、传播几何和电极-皮肤界面映射为复传递函数，评估纵向和径向配置下的衰减、相位延迟和群延迟，通过衰减纹波和延迟标准差指标量化色散引起的变异性。

Result: 在10 kHz-1 MHz范围内确认了电准静态、弱色散行为；衰减主要由几何结构驱动，而振幅纹波和延迟变异性随带宽增加而增加；界面调节（凝胶和泡沫）显著改善振幅和相位稳定性；传播几何决定链路预算和基线延迟。

Conclusion: 该框架定量地将组织电磁特性与波形失真联系起来，使可穿戴体电耦合系统能够在带宽、接口设计和收发器复杂度之间做出明智的权衡。

Abstract: This paper presents a systematic characterization of wearable galvanic coupling (GC) channels under narrowband and wideband operation. A physics-consistent digital human twin maps anatomical properties, propagation geometry, and electrode-skin interfaces into complex transfer functions directly usable for communication analysis. Attenuation, phase delay, and group delay are evaluated for longitudinal and radial configurations, and dispersion-induced variability is quantified through attenuation ripple and delay standard deviation metrics versus bandwidth. Results confirm electro-quasistatic, weakly dispersive behavior over 10 kHz-1 MHz. Attenuation is primarily geometry-driven, whereas amplitude ripple and delay variability increase with bandwidth, tightening equalization and synchronization constraints. Interface conditioning (gel and foam) significantly improves amplitude and phase stability, while propagation geometry governs link budget and baseline delay. Overall, the framework quantitatively links tissue electromagnetics to waveform distortion, enabling informed trade-offs among bandwidth, interface design, and transceiver complexity in wearable GC systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [LLMs for Human Mobility: Opportunities, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12420)
*Jie Gao,Yaoxin Wu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 这篇综述论文系统梳理了基于大语言模型的人类移动性研究，涵盖五个核心任务：旅行行程规划、轨迹生成、移动模拟、移动预测以及移动语义与理解，旨在为这一分散领域提供清晰的概览和连接框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动性研究关注人们在有意义地点之间的移动及其对可达性、拥堵、排放和公共健康的影响。虽然大语言模型越来越多地应用于该领域，能够处理地点和活动语义、旅行者意图偏好等复杂因素，但现有文献分散，缺乏将人类移动任务、挑战和LLM设计系统连接起来的清晰概览。
